# Graph Report - aulas-senai  (2026-09-05)

## Corpus Check
- 531 files · ~2,941,269 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 8802 nodes · 8893 edges · 760 communities (704 shown, 56 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 100% EXTRACTED · 0% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6 edges (avg confidence: 0.61)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `d014d76a`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
